# Phase 2 verified facts - raw query output

## Summary (filled by hand from the raw output below)
| Question | Real answer | Query # |
|---|---|---|
| Is statement `action` a list or a string? | List of strings (`List<String>`), e.g. `["iam:Get*", "iam:List*"]` | 1, 6, 17 |
| Is `resource` a list or a string? | List of strings (`List<String>`), e.g. `["*"]` | 1, 6, 17 |
| Inbound rules have fromport / toport / protocol? What do all-ports rules look like? | `protocol` is present (e.g. `"-1"`). For all-ports/all-traffic rules, `fromport` and `toport` are absent/None; ruleid format is `{groupid}/IpPermissions/NoneNone-1` | 2, 3, 13 |
| Stable id for users / roles / policies (arn or id)? | `arn` is the stable identifier across nodes and accounts; for users, roles, and policies, `id` equals `arn` | 8, 18 |
| Is `lastupdated` present? | Yes, present on all nodes as an integer epoch timestamp (e.g. `1790950275`) | 8 |
| Exact statements the Raynor user has | `effect: "Allow"`, `action: ["iam:Get*", "iam:List*", "iam:SetDefaultPolicyVersion"]`, `resource: ["*"]` from policy `cg-raynor-policy-cgidjq6epnjtgc` | 6 |
| Do S3 buckets exist, and do they carry tag properties? | Yes, S3 buckets exist (`argus-test-pii-near-pb3882`, `argus-test-pii-far-pb3882`), but tags are NOT stored as properties or relationships | 11, 12, 15 |
| Are AWS-prefixed and un-prefixed labels the same nodes (counts equal)? | Yes, exactly equal (18 `AWSEC2SecurityGroup` vs 18 `EC2SecurityGroup`); nodes have both labels attached | 9, 10 |
| Any duplicate principal ids? | None (0 duplicate IDs found) | 16 |

## Surprises (anything that differs from graph/schema.md)
- S3 Bucket tags are not synced into Neo4j (neither as `AWSTag` nodes nor as properties on `AWSS3Bucket`), so the `crown_jewel_ids` list in `risk/config.json` is required as a fallback.
- `AWSIpPermissionInbound` omits `fromport` and `toport` keys completely when all ports are allowed (protocol `-1`), encoding them as `NoneNone-1` in `ruleid`.
- Principals carry multiple labels simultaneously (e.g. `["AWSPrincipal", "AWSUser", "UserAccount"]` and `["AWSPrincipal", "AWSRole", "PermissionRole"]`).

## Query 18

```cypher
MATCH (p:AWSPrincipal) WHERE p:AWSUser OR p:AWSRole RETURN coalesce(p.arn, p.id) AS id, p.name AS name, labels(p) AS labels LIMIT 10;
```
